> **Auto-generated page** via `bash ce-cli.sh taxonomy catalog docs`.

Content developers should use these IDs when authoring YAML spec files.

| `BUNDLE_COURSE` | Curricular Planning and Pathways (Layer C1/C2) | 433 | Course bundles with curricular units |
| `BLOCK_CONTENT` | Exercises and Assessments (Layer C2) | 1269 | Content blocks: exercises and assessments |
| `PERSONA` | Agents and AI (Layer C3) | 38 | AI evaluator personalities |
| `BLOCK_INSTRUCTION` | Agents and AI (Layer C3) | 51 | Pedagogical directives |
| `BUNDLE_TOPIC` | Others | 2 | BUNDLE_TOPIC |
| `INTERACTIVE_LESSON` | Others | 6 | INTERACTIVE_LESSON |

## CLI Commands

```bash
# List all taxonomies
bash ce-cli.sh taxonomy catalog list --type TAXONOMY

# List rubrics by standard
bash ce-cli.sh taxonomy catalog list --type BLOCK_RUBRIC --root es.c0

# Show spec detail
bash ce-cli.sh taxonomy catalog show --id std.es.lomloe

# Global tree
bash ce-cli.sh taxonomy catalog tree --depth 1

# Regenerate this page
bash ce-cli.sh taxonomy catalog docs
```
